<span class="ct-node-label">Cash Landrum</span>
<span class="ct-node-title-full">What Really Happened Near Dayton?</span>
<span class="ct-node-summary">The Cash-Landrum UFO incident was a reported close encounter on 29 December 1980 near Dayton, Texas, in which Betty Cash, Vickie Landrum, and Landrum’s young grandson Colby said they saw a huge, heat-emitting, diamond-shaped object above a rural road, followed by a group of military-style helicopters.</span>
</span>
</a>
